## Weapons — why gatedBy / stage are Unknown (§7b blockers)

gatedBy derived: 47/488 (P2-S2: 0/488, before the boss registry and progression graph existed). A route is derivable only when every input in its chain is verified: boss drops (incl. treasure bags) now gate on their boss node, conditions with satisfiedByNode on their node, transitively reduced over the required-gate DAG; enemy and event-boss stages are Unknown and base materials have no acquisition data yet (P6-S0), so those chains stay underivable. Derived weapons by stage: early-prehm 0, late-prehm 7, early-hm 5, mid-hm 25, endgame 10. Each weapon below is Unknown → never "obtainable now" (A3 safe direction).

| Blockers met on every route | Weapons | Examples |
|---|---:|---|
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-conditions-unknown + drop-source-ungated + no-known-route | 174 | Iron Broadsword; Iron Shortsword; Wooden Sword |
| drop-source-ungated | 163 | Vilethorn; Starfury; Musket |
| no-known-route + vendor-conditions-unknown | 31 | Flintlock Pistol; Minishark; Ruler |
| no-known-route | 25 | Bladed Glove; Purple Clubberfish; Obsidian Swordfish |
| drop-source-ungated + vendor-conditions-unknown | 17 | Shuriken; Spiky Ball; Bomb |
| vendor-conditions-unknown | 12 | Flameburst Rod; Flameburst Cane; Flameburst Staff |
| drop-conditions-unknown + drop-source-ungated | 8 | Flower of Fire; Aqua Scepter; Ice Boomerang |
| drop-conditions-unknown | 4 | Magic Dagger; Ice Bow; KO Cannon |
| drop-source-ungated + no-known-route | 3 | Enchanted Boomerang; Trusty Cattiva; Trusty Foxparks |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-conditions-unknown | 1 | Grenade Launcher |
| alternative-route-unverified + drop-source-ungated | 1 | Slime Staff |
| drop-conditions-unknown + drop-source-ungated + vendor-conditions-unknown | 1 | Wand of Sparking |
| acquisition-withheld | 1 | Pretty Mirror |

- **no-known-route**: an input item has no recipe or drop in the datasets (base materials, vendor/fishing/chest-only items: item corpus P6-S0)
- **drop-source-ungated**: a drop comes from an enemy, an event boss or a container whose own gating is not verified (Enemy.stage Unknown; event bosses are progression markers, not Boss records; containers carry no gating data)
- **boss-attribution-pending**: a boss drop has no verified boss → progression-node attribution (source.bossId Unknown: the row's NPC ids disagree with the registry boss)
- **drop-conditions-unknown**: a drop lists its conditions as Unknown
- **vendor-conditions-unknown**: a purchase route exists but the shop availability conditions are not structured on the wiki
- **progression-graph-pending**: a gate maps to a progression node that is not in the dataset, so the stage cannot be computed
- **acquisition-withheld**: the item's acquisition is withheld because wiki sources conflict (AMENDMENTS-004 B19)
- **alternative-route-unverified**: one route is derivable, but another route is not, and it could be the less gated one — the minimum over routes is unknown (§7b)
- **cycle**: every route needs the item itself (recipe cycle)

## Bosses — stats per difficulty (Cargo NPCs) and why the rest is Unknown

hp / defense / contact damage of the representative Cargo NPCs row (pipeline/overlays/bosses.json `primary`) — the NPC's BASE stats as in the article's first infobox. Later-phase changes shown only in secondary infoboxes (e.g. second-form defense of Eye of Cthulhu, Plantera, Empress of Light) are not modeled. Bosses whose simultaneous parts have different stats (Wall of Flesh mouth/eyes, segments, Twins, Golem, Moon Lord) are Unknown rather than represented by one part. "none" = the wiki gives 0 contact damage (not applicable); Unknown = absent + listed in _prov.unverifiedFields.

## Stage-transition gates (AMENDMENTS-005 B22)

§7b stage = max over the gating nodes of (unlocksStage ?? stage). Stage-transition nodes: wall-of-flesh (late-prehm → unlocks early-hm) — each validated against a required-gate edge into a milestone of that stage. Mechanical bosses set none: one mech boss does not open mid-hm (the all-mechanical-bosses milestone does); Plantera / Golem are mid-hm nodes whose drops are mid-hm already.

Derived weapon stages without unlocksStage: early-prehm 0, late-prehm 11, early-hm 1, mid-hm 25, endgame 10. With it (emitted): early-prehm 0, late-prehm 7, early-hm 5, mid-hm 25, endgame 10.

Records whose stage moved: Breaker Blade (weapon, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Clockwork Assault Rifle (weapon, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Laser Rifle (weapon, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh); Firecracker (weapon, late-prehm → early-hm; gated by wall-of-flesh).

## Weapon Lab — archetype params and coverage (P3-S0)

Rules: pipeline/merge/labParams.ts (header = the tooling doc). Param semantics are binding as documented in src/sim/README.md ("Cadence" and "Param semantics"; AMENDMENTS-008 B37): pierce = extra dummies after the first; projectilesPerUse = projectiles per activation (bursts come from useTime < useAnimation); ammo speed only from the ammo's velocityBonusTps / velocityMultiplier; magic-bolt speed = params.projectile.velocityTps. Family = the wiki category of the weapon's article (swing-melee: Broadswords; thrust: Shortswords + Spears; bow-gun: Bows + Repeaters + Guns; magic-bolt: Wands + Magic guns + Spell books; returning: Boomerangs + Flails + Yoyos; whip: Whips; minion-sentry: Minion summon items + Sentry summon items; explosive-aoe: Explosives + Launchers; piercing-beam: no wiki category — the held-beam data signal). "Derived" = every param computed from the data modules / sprite metadata and the article screen found nothing the params cannot express; "overlay" = the screen flagged the weapon and a reviewed entry in pipeline/overlays/lab-params.json (verbatim quotes, checked against the cached article revision) decided it; "downgraded" = params were assigned but a core field the sim needs is unverified, so the validate stage's §2.1-3 rule (incl. AMENDMENTS-008 B36) emits a limitation. Every emitted archetype weapon passed src/sim assessWeapon (build-time guard contract).

Families since the remaining-archetype slice: returning (Boomerangs + Flails + Yoyos), whip (Whips), minion-sentry (Minion + Sentry summon items), explosive-aoe (Explosives + Launchers) and piercing-beam (no wiki category — the held-beam data signal: held magic/ranged weapons outside every category family). Their sim interpreters ship with the Lab (P3-S6–S11): "params complete, pending interpreter" weapons have every param recorded below but are emitted as limitations naming what the Lab does not model (the guard contract above holds; the same build emits their params once `IMPLEMENTED_ARCHETYPES` includes the archetype).

| Family (archetype) | Weapons | Derived | Overlay (reviewed) | Params complete, pending interpreter (core field unverified) | Downgraded at validate | Limitation (of which: a param missing) | **Simulable** | Simulable % |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| swing-melee | 75 | 22 | 15 | 0 (0) | 0 | 38 (0) | **37** | 49.3% |
| thrust | 31 | 5 | 13 | 0 (0) | 0 | 13 (0) | **18** | 58.1% |
| bow-gun | 67 | 27 | 11 | 0 (0) | 0 | 29 (0) | **38** | 56.7% |
| magic-bolt | 62 | 0 | 3 | 0 (0) | 0 | 59 (0) | **3** | 4.8% |
| returning | 51 | 0 | 22 | 0 (0) | 0 | 29 (0) | **22** | 43.1% |
| whip | 18 | 0 | 6 | 0 (0) | 0 | 12 (0) | **6** | 33.3% |
| minion-sentry | 46 | 0 | 10 | 0 (0) | 0 | 36 (7) | **10** | 21.7% |
| explosive-aoe | 27 | 0 | 15 | 15 (15) | 0 | 12 (0) | **0** | 0.0% |
| piercing-beam | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 (0) | 0 | 3 (0) | **0** | 0.0% |

| Family | Limitation reason | Weapons |
|---|---|---:|
| swing-melee | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 38 |
| thrust | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 11 |
| thrust | game data is not a plain stab (use style / held use / projectile is not the stab) | 2 |
| bow-gun | reviewed: the article documents behavior the archetype params cannot express (overlay) | 29 |

**AMENDMENTS-008 B36** (simRequiredWeaponFields += useAnimationTicks for every archetype, + autoReuse for swing-melee / thrust / returning): of 149 weapons given params, 0 are downgraded by the validate rule; 0 of them only because of the B36 fields (none). Weapons in the whole dataset with an unverified B36 field: Inferno Fork (autoReuse; magic-bolt → limitation); Starlight (autoReuse; thrust → limitation) — none of them had params to lose (a thrust limitation and a reviewed magic limitation), so B36 changes no emitted record in this build; it now guarantees that any future params on them are downgraded with a proper limitation card. B36 adds requirements only, so it upgrades nothing; the slice's upgrades are the 134 weapons whose labBehavior went from the P2 default limitation to a simulable archetype. Guard-only refusals turned into limitations at merge time: none.

**Coverage expansion (AMENDMENTS-010 B48; AMENDMENTS-012 B56 stated thrust ranges: thrust +9)** — simulable vs the P3-S0 baseline (swing-melee 35 · thrust 0 · bow-gun 34 · magic-bolt 2 = 71): swing-melee **37** (+2) · thrust **18** (+18) · bow-gun **38** (+4) · magic-bolt **3** (+1) = **96** (+25). Every P3-S0 archetype weapon kept its params; B49 adds `_prov.modeledFields` to the convention-derived ones.

| Modeling convention (src/schemas MODELING_CONVENTIONS) | Ruling | Archetype | Modeled param paths (`_prov.modeledFields`) | Emitted weapons listing them | Params complete, pending interpreter (recorded) |
|---|---|---|---|---:|---:|
| swing-geometry: arc 180° (broadswords are swung over the head); reach = item sprite diagonal × item scale ÷ 16 px | AMENDMENTS-010 B44 | swing-melee | labBehavior.params.arcDegrees, labBehavior.params.reachTiles (every record of the archetype) | 37 | 0 |
| thrust-reach: reach = item sprite diagonal × item scale ÷ 16 px + 1 tile arm extension, only where the article states no range (AMENDMENTS-012 B56) | AMENDMENTS-010 B46 | thrust | labBehavior.params.reachTiles | 9 | 0 |
| slight-spread: a gun whose article says it fires at a "slight" / "small" spread without an angle uses a 10° spread | AMENDMENTS-010 B47 | bow-gun | labBehavior.params.spreadDegrees | 3 | 0 |
| whip-arc: whip arc 180° forward (the B44 "swung overhead" basis; the Whips article states no angle) | AMENDMENTS-013 B63 P-1 | whip | labBehavior.params.arcDegrees (every record of the archetype) | 6 | 0 |
| summon-average-rate: attack interval = the rate the summon's article states as an average / random cooldown, or in seconds that are not whole ticks, × 60 rounded half-to-even to whole ticks | AMENDMENTS-013 B63 P-2 | minion-sentry | labBehavior.params.attackRateTicks | 4 | 0 |
| summon-hit-cap: a contact minion whose article states only its immunity frames attacks once per that many ticks — an UPPER BOUND (maximum rate; actual depends on minion AI pathing) | AMENDMENTS-013 B63 P-3 | minion-sentry | labBehavior.params.attackRateTicks | 6 | 0 |
| square-blast-radius: a square damage area ("within N tiles horizontally and vertically") → the inscribed circle, radius = half the side = N tiles (conservative: never overstates the area) | AMENDMENTS-013 B63 P-5 | explosive-aoe | labBehavior.params.blastRadiusTiles | 0 | 5 |

modeledFields sample: Iron Broadsword (i:4) → ["labBehavior.params.arcDegrees","labBehavior.params.reachTiles"]; Iron Shortsword (i:6) → ["labBehavior.params.reachTiles"]; Minishark (i:98) → ["labBehavior.params.spreadDegrees"]. The validate stage requires every listed path to be present and governed by a convention for the record's archetype, and every swing-melee / whip record to list its convention paths (a thrust record lists its reach only when it comes from the B46 convention — AMENDMENTS-012 B56).

**Reuse delay (B48(a))**: 33 weapons carry `reuseDelayTicks` (Iteminfo reuseDelay > 0; the sim adds it after each use — wiki Use time: "reuseDelay is applied after the itemAnimation counter turns to zero"): Clockwork Assault Rifle 14 (simulated), Pygmy Staff 2, Slime Staff 2 (simulated), Raven Staff 2, Hornet Staff 2, Imp Staff 2 (simulated), Optic Staff 2, Spider Staff 2, Pirate Staff 2 (simulated), Tempest Staff 2, Xeno Staff 2 (simulated), Deadly Sphere Staff 2, Medusa Head 10, Stardust Cell Staff 2, Stardust Dragon Staff 2, Last Prism 5, Sky Fracture 18, Betsy's Wrath 10, Sanguine Staff 2, Vampire Frog Staff 2 (simulated), Finch Staff 2 (simulated), Desert Tiger Staff 2, Blade Staff 2, Terraprisma 2 (simulated), Flinx Staff 2 (simulated), Abigail's Flower 2, Mushroom Staff 2, Cattiva 2, Foxparks 2, Trusty Cattiva 2, Trusty Foxparks 2, Clay Bud Staff 2 (simulated), Ruinous Staff 2 (simulated).

**Damage over time from the infobox (B48(c))**: modifiers.dot derived for 9 simulated weapons — Flamarang: on-fire 50% for 180 ticks; Ice Boomerang: frostburn 50% for 180 ticks; Frost Staff: frostbite 100% for 420 ticks; Imp Staff: on-fire 100% for {"minTicks":180,"maxTicks":360} ticks; Yelets: acid-venom 100% for 120 ticks; Amarok: frostbite 67% for {"minTicks":120,"maxTicks":300} ticks; Hel-Fire: hellfire 100% for {"minTicks":180,"maxTicks":480} ticks; Stylish Scissors: bleeding 100% for {"minTicks":240,"maxTicks":360} ticks; Brand of the Inferno: hellfire 25% for 300 ticks. Underivable damaging debuffs (limitation): none (every such weapon also has a reviewed limitation for other behavior).

**Per-projectile damage multipliers (B48(b))**: `ProjectileSpec.damageMultiplierPct` is supported end to end (schema, sim rounding ⌊damage × pct / 100⌋ as in the wiki's own figures, quote-checked overlay residual incl. a "(N base damage)" cross-check). Emitted on 0 weapons: every family weapon whose article states a per-projectile percentage also has behavior the Lab cannot express, so it stays a reviewed limitation — True Night's Edge, Sapphire Staff, Ruby Staff, Mushroom Spear, Chlorophyte Saber, Golem Fist, Ice Sickle, Obsidian Swordfish, Anchor, Amber Staff, Storm Spear, Sergeant United Shield, Mushroom Staff.

**B46 thrust reach vs the range the article states (calibration record)**: AMENDMENTS-010 B46 rests on the thrust reach being undocumented, but 19 of the 31 thrust-family weapons' articles state a range ("maximum range of approximately N tiles"; some include a tip shockwave). AMENDMENTS-012 B56: where the article states a range, that range is the reach — an extracted wiki value whose sentence is checked verbatim on the cached revision (normal article provenance, not in `_prov.modeledFields`); the convention applies only where none is stated. Simulated with a stated range: Adamantite Glaive 11.03, Cobalt Naginata 10.5, Mythril Halberd 9.45, Orichalcum Halberd 8.97, Palladium Pike 10.23, Spear 5.05, Swordfish 5.65, Titanium Trident 10.44, Trident 5.73 (9); with the B46 convention: 9. **Article screen** (high recall; a flag only routes a weapon to review, it never sets a value): projectile — fires or creates projectiles (swing-melee, thrust); extra-projectile — fires or creates projectiles (returning, whip, explosive-aoe); multi-projectile — several projectiles per shot (bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning); spread — spread or randomized aim (bow-gun, magic-bolt); conversion — converts or replaces its ammunition (bow-gun, magic-bolt); homing — homing (bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, whip, explosive-aoe, piercing-beam); bounce — bouncing (bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, explosive-aoe); pierce — piercing (bow-gun, magic-bolt); cursor — steered toward the cursor (returning); on-hit — extra effects on hit (swing-melee, thrust, bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, whip, minion-sentry, explosive-aoe, piercing-beam); spawns — creates other projectiles or entities (swing-melee, thrust, bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, whip, explosive-aoe, piercing-beam); conditional-damage — damage that depends on conditions (swing-melee, thrust, bow-gun, magic-bolt, returning, whip, minion-sentry, explosive-aoe, piercing-beam); motion — non-straight projectile motion (bow-gun, magic-bolt); special-use — charged, held or alternate use (swing-melee, thrust, bow-gun, magic-bolt, whip, minion-sentry, explosive-aoe); the tooltip is screened with the same rules; plus data flags: shoots-projectile (Iteminfo shoot > 0, melee), infobox-projectile, inflicts-debuff (Cargo debuffs / infobox buff), burst (useTime < useAnimation). Hard (non-overridable) data rules: use style / class / ammo / mana / channel signals per archetype; thrust: the Iteminfo projectile must be the stab itself (Projectileinfo name = weapon name); the reach is the range the article states (AMENDMENTS-012 B56) or, where none is stated, the B46 convention; a debuff with damage over time must be derivable from the infobox debuff rows (B48(c)). Since B48(a) Iteminfo reuseDelay is part of the sim cadence (no longer a limitation). Flag counts among family weapons: on-hit 241, conditional-damage 161, spawns 151, inflicts-debuff 91, multi-projectile 86, infobox-projectile 70, pierce 68, motion 41, bounce 39, projectile 37, special-use 36, spread 31, conversion 30, homing 28, shoots-projectile 27, extra-projectile 22, burst 13.

**Param provenance (remaining families)** — stated: the yoyo infobox "Reach" row (cross-checked against the Yoyos list table "Reach (tiles)"), the Whips list table "Range (tiles)" / "Tag damage" / "Multihit penalty", the whip tooltip (game text) "N summon tag damage" / "N% summon tag critical strike chance", the verbatim article sentences "maximum range of … N tiles" (boomerangs, launched flails; AMENDMENTS-012 B56 extraction) and "explodes N seconds after being thrown" (fuse), and reviewed overlay quotes that state the value (launched-flail ranges in other wordings, blast radius, uniform damage = falloff none, minion AI class, beam hit rate); derived: returnMode / control from the Yoyos / Boomerangs / Flails rule quotes + Iteminfo `channel` (thrown vs launched flail), minion slotCost = Projectileinfo `minionSlots`, sentry slotCost 1 (rule quote); stated summon rates: the article's one base-rate sentence ("once every N seconds", "a cooldown of N seconds after attacking") when it is whole ticks and not an average; modeled (AMENDMENTS-013 B63, below): whip arcDegrees (P-1), other stated summon rates (P-2) or a contact minion's immunity-frame cap (P-3) as attackRateTicks, a square damage area as blastRadiusTiles (P-5). **Convention rulings applied (AMENDMENTS-013 B63)**: each param a ruling supplies is MODELED — recorded in the decision's `modeled` list (src/schemas MODELING_CONVENTIONS; a path two conventions share is qualified `<path>#<convention id>`), emitted as `_prov.modeledFields` and labeled by the sim's approximations registry (AssumptionsTip) once the archetype is simulated — never presented as game data. "Applied" counts every family member whose recorded param the convention supplied (reviewed limitations included); "params complete" = those now complete (pending their interpreter). The B63 conventions complete 5 weapon(s): Bouncy Dynamite, Dynamite, Power Bomb, Sticky Dynamite, Sticky Power Bomb.

| Ruling | Convention (MODELING_CONVENTIONS id) | Param | Rule | Applied | Params complete (pending interpreter) | Examples (value) |
|---|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| B63 P-1 | whip-arc | arcDegrees | whip arc 180° forward (the B44 "swung overhead" basis; the Whips article states no angle) | 18 | 0 | Cobwhip 180; Constellation 180; Cool Whip 180; Dark Harvest 180; Durendal 180; Electric Eel 180 |
| B63 P-2 | summon-average-rate | attackRateTicks | summon attack rate from an article-stated average / random cooldown, or a time that is not whole ticks — × 60, rounded half-to-even to ticks | 13 | 0 | Ballista Cane 160; Ballista Rod 160; Ballista Staff 160; Barnacle Staff 50; Deadly Sphere Staff 32; Hornet Staff 45 |
| B63 P-3 | summon-hit-cap | attackRateTicks | a contact minion's stated immunity frames as its attack rate — an UPPER BOUND (maximum rate; actual depends on minion AI pathing); not applied where the article says the frames do not come into play | 6 | 0 | Clay Bud Staff 30; Finch Staff 15; Flinx Staff 10; Pirate Staff 18; Slime Staff 12; Vampire Frog Staff 10 |
| B63 P-5 | square-blast-radius | blastRadiusTiles | a square damage area ("within N tiles horizontally and vertically") → its inscribed circle, radius N = half the side (conservative) | 5 | 5 | Bouncy Dynamite 7.8; Dynamite 7.8; Power Bomb 8.75; Sticky Dynamite 7.8; Sticky Power Bomb 8.75 |
| B63 P-4 | — (REJECTED) | sentryRangeTiles | a Manhattan-distance sentry range is not a radius: stays missing | 0 | 0 | stated as a Manhattan distance: Houndius Shootius, Queen Spider Staff, Staff of the Frost Hydra |
| B63 P-6 | — (taxonomy, BINDING) | aiClass | ranged-shot = it damages enemies with projectiles it shoots, fires, spits or throws; ground-melee = it moves along the ground (walks, runs, hops, bounces, jumps) and hits enemies up close (contact, sword, slam, bite); melee-contact = it flies, charges or dashes at enemies, damaging them by contact; hover-strike = it flies or hovers next to enemies and strikes them repeatedly at close range (stab, slash, swing) (labFamilies.ts header) | 17 classified | 0 | — |

**Still incomplete after B63 (7; a missing param is never guessed)**: Ballista Cane — missing sentryRangeTiles; Ballista Rod — missing sentryRangeTiles; Ballista Staff — missing sentryRangeTiles; Barnacle Staff — missing sentryRangeTiles; Houndius Shootius — missing attackRateTicks, sentryRangeTiles (sentryRangeTiles: Manhattan distance 62.5 tiles ("The Houndius Shootius can target enemies within a Manhattan distance of 62.5 tiles")); Pygmy Staff — missing attackRateTicks; Raven Staff — missing attackRateTicks (attackRateTicks: the article says its immunity frames do not limit its hits ("The Raven minion applies 10 ticks of local invincibility frames upon hitting an enemy, although in practice this does not come into play due to the internal …") — B63 P-3 not applied).

**Schema deltas surfaced (not applied; for the schema owner)**: (S-1) returning params carry no count of projectiles that may be out at once — Bananarang (10), Trimarang (3), Light Disc (6) and Possessed Hatchet (unlimited) are reviewed limitations for it, while the Boomerangs article states "Most boomerangs cannot be fired again before returning to the player" (the interpreter's default); (S-2) yoyo spin duration (infobox "Spin duration", Yoyos table) has no returning param — it bounds how long a yoyo stays out; (S-3) explosive-aoe carries no ammo class — all launchers are limitations for it; (S-4) whips: Possession "swings four times per use" (no swings-per-use param).

**Sim-side follow-ups (the Lab owns src/sim; not changed here)**: (a) summon weapons have `critPct` not applicable (wiki: summon damage cannot normally crit — absent, not listed: 64 whip / minion / sentry weapons), but the guard's COMMON_REQUIRED_FIELDS requires critPct, so P3-S9 / P3-S10 must treat summon crit as not applicable or every summon weapon is refused as missing-required-field; (b) every thrown explosive has damage / critPct / knockback Unknown (Iteminfo damage 0 vs the infobox — a withheld source mismatch), so the 15 complete explosives would be downgraded at validate until that is resolved; (c) boomerang / launched-flail cadence: most cannot be re-thrown before returning (Boomerangs article; KO Cannon: "its fire rate is dependent only on the distance to its impact").

**Recorded for later (quote-checked, never emitted; the bounce / chain sim stubs throw NotImplemented, so these weapons stay limitations with their specific reason)**: Amber Staff bounceCount 2; Beam Sword bounceCount 3; Candy Corn Rifle bounceCount 4; Cursed Flames bounceCount 4; Flower of Fire bounceCount 5; Flower of Frost bounceCount 7; Last Prism tickRateTicks 5; Meowmere bounceCount 4; Pulse Bow bounceCount 5. Chain targets: none quotable among the family articles (no "chains to N enemies" statement).

| − set rows (type "armor" + "set" = the set infobox of a set article, not an item; 70 set articles) | 77 |
| = armor piece rows | 256 |
| **= armor pieces emitted** | **256** (reconciles) |
|   head / chest / legs | 105 / 81 / 70 |
|   pieces of an emitted set (setId) / without a set | 228 / 28 |
| Module:Iteminfo/data non-vanity items with exactly one head/body/leg slot | 256 |
|   = emitted armor pieces (every emitted piece is one) + Iteminfo-only | 256 + 0 (reconciles) |

**Wiki category tree** — Category:Armor items: 3 categories (Armor items, Armor sets, Armor sets with no set bonus), 97 distinct articles.

|---|---:|

## Armor sets — set articles, class-variant helmets, set bonuses (P2-S8)

77 Cargo set rows on 70 set articles → **67 armor sets emitted** + 3 withheld by a reviewed, quote-checked pipeline/overlays/armor-sets.json entry (Beetle armor, Rain armor, Wizard set; one count-reconciliation gap each, their pieces carry no setId) (reconciles). Articles with several set rows: Wizard set 8. Validate checks every set as a hard §7a-6 rule: pieces resolve with the matching slot, piece setId back-refs agree (E_DERIVED_MISMATCH), helmets are not repeated and variants ⇄ helmets are bijective (94 variants = 94 helmets).

Category check: set articles not in a Category:Armor sets* category: none; Category:Armor sets* articles without a Cargo set row: none.

Set Defense totals (infobox, per helmet or one total) vs Σ piece defense (each piece Iteminfo, cross-checked): 77 agree, 0 mismatch, 17 without a total. Variant class (editorialFields) = the damage-class signals of helmet + chest + legs + set bonus stats; hybrid sets are ruled "other" by a quoted overlay entry. Set stage = earliest stage a complete set can be assembled, Unknown unless every piece stage is derived (2/67 derived).

## Equipment effects — closed StatKind classification and Equipinfo cross-check (P2-S8)

Every game-text line (Cargo Items `tooltip` for pieces and accessories, the set infobox "Set Bonus" for sets) matches exactly one rule in pipeline/merge/effects.ts — an unmatched or doubly-matched line fails the build (StatKind is closed). A stat rule's numbers must all occur in the line; specials keep the verbatim line (simulated: false, sourceUrl = the record's article). 103 rules; lines classified: armor pieces + set bonuses 439, accessories 590.

## StatKind extension candidates (amendment proposals, P2-S8)

Effects the closed StatKind enum (DATA_SCHEMA_SPEC §2.3) cannot express are emitted as `special` (simulated: false) — never as an invented stat. These rules name the StatKind extension that would make them structured; each row is wiki evidence (verbatim game text + article) for an amendment.

"Existing kind" = the StatKind is in the enum but the game text states no value / unit / scope for it (a unit definition, a value source or a scope extension is the amendment); "new kind" = no StatKind covers it.

## Tinkering — combinedFrom / upgradesTo derived from Tinkerer's Workshop recipes (§7a-7, P2-S8)

Rule (validate/integrity.ts deriveTinkering, the one definition the build and validate share): a recipe whose station is the Tinkerer's Workshop (item 398) and whose result is an emitted accessory links each ITEM ingredient that is itself an emitted accessory; other stations, group ingredients ("Any …") and materials never link. 151 Tinkerer's Workshop recipes; 137 produce an emitted accessory → 257 combinedFrom links = 257 upgradesTo links (reconciles); 114 accessories have combinedFrom, 167 have upgradesTo. Validate re-derives both directions and fails on any difference (E_DERIVED_MISMATCH) — symmetric by construction and by check.

- Recipes with a group ingredient (the group member is not linked — groups never link): Bundle of Horseshoe Balloons; Bundle of Horseshoe Balloons; Bundle of Horseshoe Balloons
- Two-way links (each is a Tinkerer's Workshop recipe on the wiki; consumers walking the tree must cycle-guard): Band of Starpower ↔ Panic Necklace

## Sprites

public/sprites/manifest.json lists 488/488 weapon sprites, 18/18 boss sprites, 79/79 ammo item sprites, 256/256 armor piece sprites and 352/352 accessory sprites (16 animated GIFs), each with its wiki File: page, SHA-1, size and rights "re-logic"; 0 weapons, 0 armor pieces and 0 accessories have no wiki image and use the placeholder. Binaries are downloaded locally by `npm run pipeline:sprites` (verified against imageinfo — exact SHA-1 or CDN original size, see #8; gitignored, never in CI — A10); fallbackMode=false.

Thumbnails (AMENDMENTS-007 B30): every sprite file over 100 KB gets a static PNG of its first frame, downscaled by a whole number to ≤ 128 px (nearest-neighbour block sampling — pixel art is never resampled fractionally), generated locally by `npm run pipeline:sprites`: /sprites/bosses/113.gif (boss:wall-of-flesh, 668777 B, 210×420) → /sprites/thumbs/bosses/113.png 53×105; /sprites/bosses/396.gif (boss:moon-lord, 427536 B, 191×150) → /sprites/thumbs/bosses/396.png 96×75; /sprites/bosses/636.gif (boss:empress-of-light, 677616 B, 386×218) → /sprites/thumbs/bosses/636.png 97×55. Heavy files without a thumbnail (generator decodes GIF only): none. Rows and cards should use the thumbnail, the full file only on detail / boss pages (UI wiring: next UI slice).

## TO-VERIFY-IN-P2 resolutions

- **#4 NPCs table per-difficulty hp/defense** — Cargo NPCs `life` / `defense` / `damage` are rendered infobox cells: a per-difficulty block (`npcstat` or `mode-content` with m-normal / m-expert / m-master / m-expert-master / m-all spans), or a bare integer for every difficulty (6 of 1,538 cells). Additional lines are alternative values (enraged, per-health, with minions) or — after a "(contact)" note — projectile damages; cells can carry the wiki's "based on outdated versions of Terraria's source code" category flag. pipeline/parse/npcStats.ts keeps only one integer per difficulty and withholds everything else; a boss difficulty is emitted only with both hp and defense verified (6/18 bosses).
- **#1 Cargo Items columns / class / rarity** — Cargo Items has 43 columns (itemid, name, internalname, image, imagefile, imageplaced, imageequipped, autoswing, stack, consumable, hardmode, type, listcat, tag, damage, damagetype, defense, velocity, knockback, research, rare, buy, sell, axe, pick, hammer, fishing, bait, bonus, toolspeed, usetime, unobtainable, critical, tooltip, placeable, placedwidth, placedheight, mana, hheal, mheal, bodyslot, buffs, debuffs). `type` is a ^-list of infobox types ("weapon", "tool", "ammunition", …); `damagetype` is the class as text (Melee/Ranged/Magic/Summon, empty for classless) and agrees with the Iteminfo class flags for every identified weapon (455 agree, 0 mismatch, 33 with no wiki class: 32 classless in both sources, 1 with an Iteminfo class only); `rare` is the game rarity number (plain or wrapped in the rarity template "Rarity level: N"; 488/488 weapons agree with Iteminfo); `critical` = 4 + Iteminfo crit (391 agree, 0 mismatch); `usetime` = Iteminfo useAnimation; `velocity` is the effective projectile velocity (equals Iteminfo shootSpeed for 330 weapons, differs for 95 — extra updates / ammo), so it is not cross-checked. `tag` carries vendor:<NPC> (purchase routes), `listcat` route hints; `stack` reports 9999 for weapons (B7 — maxStack only for consumable weapons, from Iteminfo).
- **#9 special rarity tiers** — Module:Iteminfo/data `rare`: -1 gray, 0–11 numeric tiers, -11 amber (quest fish + Strange Plants), -12 rainbow (Expert items; flag expert:true, base tier in `rarity`), -13 fiery red (Master items). Cargo `rare` uses the same numbers (Shield of Cthulhu -12, Eye of Cthulhu Relic -13). Mapped to Rarity -1..11 | "amber" | "rainbow" | "fiery-red"; 0 weapons use a special tier (weapon rarities span 0–10).
- **#10 useTime vs useAnimation** — The wiki's "Use time" equals Iteminfo useAnimation for 488/488 weapons with a wiki value (useTime differs from the wiki value for 42, e.g. Zenith useTime 10 / useAnimation 30). useAnimation = length of one use (attack cycle); useTime = interval between successive uses/shots within it (bursts, multi-swing projectiles). Emitted: useTimeTicks = useTime, useAnimationTicks = useAnimation (both always present). Display "Use time" from useAnimationTicks (wiki parity); attack cycles per second = 60 / useAnimationTicks; useAnimationTicks was added to the weapons list projection for that reason.
- **#11 ammo damage / velocity modifier location (RESOLVED, P2-S7)** — A weapon's ammo class is Iteminfo `useAmmo` = the game's AmmoID, i.e. the item ID of the class's representative item (40 Wooden Arrow, 97 Musket Ball, 771 Rocket I, 23 Gel …); ammo items carry the same value in `ammo` plus their own `damage`, `knockBack` and `shootSpeed`, which the game adds to the weapon's (Cargo Items `damage` / `knockback` / `velocity` = the same base values: 64 / 59 / 51 ammo records agree, 0 mismatches). ammoClass = slug of the representative item's name (17 classes among weapons: acorn, ale, candy-corn, copper-coin, explosive-jack-o-lantern, fallen-star, flare, gel, musket-ball, nail, rocket-i, sand-block, seed, snowball, stake, stynger-bolt, wooden-arrow; every one matched by ≥1 ammo record — §7a-10 is a hard check again). The velocity MULTIPLIER (B2) is the ammo projectile's `extraUpdates` + 1: Module:Projectileinfo/data rev 1030091 (generated 2026-09-13 15:45 (+00:00)) carries extraUpdates per projectile, and the wiki's item infobox shows "Velocity Multiplier N×" (linked to Velocity#extraUpdates) — emitted from the item's own infobox, cross-checked with Projectileinfo (42 agree, 0 mismatch). Iteminfo `shoot` is NOT always a projectile for ammo (rockets and solutions store a type index: Rocket II "shoots" projectile 3), so Projectileinfo is never joined without the infobox naming the projectile. Per-weapon ammo conversions (Uzi → High Velocity Bullets, …) are weapon behaviors (P3 archetype params).
- **#12 structured buff-ID source (RESOLVED, P2-S7)** — The wiki article "Buff IDs" (rev 1029302) is a table of every game buff: ID, name, internal name and type (Buff / Debuff), each name linking its article (352 named rows; potion buffs link their potion's page, e.g. Ironskin → Ironskin Potion). Buff articles repeat the ID in their infobox ("Buff ID" on buff pages, "Internal Buff ID" on potion/weapon pages) — 39 agree, 0 mismatch, 0 articles state none. Iteminfo `buffType` (the buff an item grants) is the same ID (Ale → 25 Tipsy). One name can cover several IDs (Zapped 399 / 400) → gameBuffId Unknown. Debuff articles state damage over time in their infobox "Effects" row per target ("−24 health per second (on enemies)" / "(on players)"; unqualified = every entity) → Buff.dot.dps is the enemy value.
- **D1 caveat: 1.4.5.7 → 1.4.5.8 patch diff** — The 1.4.5.8 patch notes (wiki page "1.4.5.8", rev 1029883) name three weapons — Clay Bud Staff (now always knocks enemies away), Last Prism (laser fades without enough mana), Acorn Slingshot (crash fix) — all behavioral; no numeric item stat changed, so no extracted field is in the patch-diff set. The Cargo cross-check above is the re-confirmation for every compared field.
- **critPct for summon weapons** — The wiki's Critical hit page (rev 1032172) states summon damage cannot normally deal critical hits, and the infobox shows no crit for all 64 summon weapons; critPct is therefore not applicable (absent, not listed). Classless weapons show no crit either but no rule is documented → Unknown.
- **#5 Equipinfo columns (defense, tooltip, wing flight time) (RESOLVED, P2-S8)** — Cargo Equipinfo (184 columns, 503 rows on Template:GetEquipInfo/db) is the wiki's transcription of the game's UpdateEquip effect code per item: class damage as fractions (meleeDamage "+0.1"), crit / mana / minion slots as integers, moveSpeed, endurance, pickSpeed, whipRangeMultiplier, flags (noKnockback, fireWalk, buffImmune lists, accRunSpeed …). It has NO defense, tooltip or wing flight-time column: defense comes from Module:Iteminfo/data `defense` (cross-checked with the Cargo Items infobox: armor 253 agree / 0 mismatch; accessories 16 / 0), the tooltip from Cargo Items `tooltip` (game text), and flight time from the "Wings" article list table (Iteminfo `wingSlot` only names the wing). 19 Equipinfo rows carry no item ID (effect code shared by several items). Equipinfo is therefore the cross-check of the tooltip-derived stats, not their source.
- **#2 recipe-group encoding** — Cargo Recipes stores a group ingredient as its plain English name ("¦Any Iron Bar¦" in `ingredients`, "Any Iron Bar¦2" in `args`, "¦Any Iron Bar¦2" in `ings`); members are not in Cargo. Members come from Module:Recipes `group("Any …", {names})` (what the {{recipes}} template uses), resolved to IDs via Module:Iteminfo/data and cross-checked against Module:RecipeGroups (the game's SetupRecipeGroups, v1.4.5.4 transcription). Station alternatives (B3) come from Module:Recipes/l10n `en.station_cate` ("Iron Anvil" → "Iron or Lead Anvil").
- **#3 drop NPC ids / multi-part bosses** — Cargo Drops `id` carries NPC ids for most NPC rows (single, comma lists, ranges, per-variant notes, negative variant netIDs); container rows (crates, chests, treasure bags) carry none or the boss id. Ids are cross-checked against Cargo NPCs by name (mismatches withheld + gap); empty ids are filled from NPCs. Multi-part bosses: every part/segment NPC is typed `boss Part` and lives on the boss article (`_page`), so drops attribute to that boss. P2-S6: the boss registry resolves each boss-typed / treasure-bag row by its article (bossId, cross-checked against the boss's NPC ids); rows on event-boss articles get no bossId (not applicable — events are markers, A3).
- **#8 Imageinfo → sprite file resolution** — Cargo Items `imagefile` names the infobox image ("Zenith.png"); `prop=imageinfo&iiprop=url|size|sha1|mime` on "File:<imagefile>" (50 titles per request) returns the original file URL on terraria.wiki.gg/images/, its SHA-1, size and dimensions; the URL's ?<6 hex> key is the SHA-1 prefix (pins the file version). The wiki's CDN (Cloudflare Polish, `cf-polished: ok, orig_size=N`) serves most PNGs re-encoded, so a byte-exact SHA-1 match is only possible for some files; downloads are accepted when the SHA-1 matches exactly OR the CDN-reported original size equals the wiki's recorded size of that version (pipeline:sprites reports both counts).
- **#13 Cargo paging** — limit=5000 requested; the server returned at most 500 rows per page for every table (Recipes 9 pages, Drops 7, NPCs 2, Items 13, Weapon_source 3). Pagination is by offset ordered by _ID until COUNT(*) is reached.
